**Amendment (Paper case declaration admission)** · `amend:identity:paper-case-declaration` Admit PaperCaseDeclaration, component edition 1, for publication version three only under (`req:identity:admission-record`) and (`gate:identity:implementation`). pn-records owns this reusable descriptor, deployment selection produces it and the epoch comparison consumer compares its complete value against an independently authenticated in-force declaration; pn-host owns its canonical fragment codec. The benefit is an unambiguous statement of which external predicate is selected, not proof of its truth. Schema: [1,Ref,kind,scope,second_party,rules,convention,projection,data_selection]. Ref is [namespace,name,edition], with unsigned 64-bit edition, nonempty namespace in (`gram:interchange:label-grammar`) and name one atom of that grammar, each at most 255 UTF-8 bytes; historical DeclarationId values are not narrowed. Kind remains unsigned 0 for the verifiable-provenance declaration case and 1 for the author-authority declaration case admitted here; the local extension is neither. These are historical local component meanings, not selectable declaration cases required by network-v0.37.4. Scope is [run,history_scope,announced_at,effective_at], run exactly 32 bytes, epoch values unsigned 64-bit with announcement no later than effect. History_scope is a nonempty byte string retaining the exact already-canonical public starting-context bytes supplied by the independently selected history; it is compared byte for byte, never hashed, normalized or substituted by a locator. Its full publication placement and authentication remain C7P obligations. Second_party, rules, convention and projection are Ref values; data_selection is [published_data_rule_ref,unit], with unit a Ref for kind 0 and null for kind 1. Unit names the second party's unit of destruction. The rule edition is that party's rules, while convention names the deployment interpretation. Definitions are resolved against the selected immutable public editions: conflicting content under one Ref refuses DeclarationContent; unobtainable text gives UnavailableEvaluation. The scope and distinctions use realizes{``def:comparator:burn-provenance-oracle``; This admits a bounded declaration representation and its comparison, not a selected external case or its truth.} and realizes{``def:graph:author-authority-oracle``; This admits a bounded declaration representation and its comparison, not a selected external case or its truth.}. No production paper case is selected here. Its pure typed producer, fragment codec and conditional comparison consumer now have finite representation and order controls; independently authenticated selection and a production paper-case evaluator remain owed. The act-and-epoch document concept (`spec:actepoch:concept`) decides the designed and unbuilt successor for the document subjects stated here; this carrier keeps all its required material and admitted meaning until its owners admit the replacement in the successor document admission cut.

The following common representation and comparison discipline is part of each of these five admissions; pn-host owns the canonical fragment codec for all five, while each record names its typed owner, producer and comparison consumer. The caller selects the version-three component operation and supported component edition before any decoding, never by sniffing candidate bytes; historical readers are unchanged. Use canonical definite-length shortest-head CBOR and full consumption without repair, as in (`amend:identity:closure-components`). No maps, floats, tags or indefinite forms occur in these new schemas. H(n) is 1, 2, 3, 5 or 9 for nonnegative n below 24, 256, 65,536, 4,294,967,296 or 18,446,744,073,709,551,616. E(uint)=H(uint), E(null)=1, E(bytes/text of n bytes)=H(n)+n and E(array)=H(count)+sum E(member); text sizes count UTF-8 bytes. All arithmetic is checked, all counts/indexes are u64. Context is [run,history_scope,epoch]; PriorRange is [history_scope,0,epoch], a half-open reference to the complete supplied authenticated earlier epoch record, not a URI. Every standalone reader has independently supplied finite allowances for total encoded bytes, starting-context bytes, declarations, transitions, account bytes, blocks, block bytes, parts, associations and prior-history traversal; their quantity, stage and nested treatment are (`rule:identity:component-allowance-order`), and length checks precede allocation. These allowances express ability to read, not authority or publication Work, and their exhaustion returns UnavailableBudget directly to the operation's caller, including exhaustion inside a nested component; it is never Field or any other input fault. There is no arbitrary fixed account-size ceiling or reuse of the dependency-opening ceiling. Existing nested V, material, act and signature/key limits remain exactly admitted. A component's exact size is E of its stated complete array; thus this descriptor is H(9)+H(1)+E(Ref)+H(kind)+E(scope)+E(second_party)+E(rules)+E(convention)+E(projection)+E(data_selection). The admission retains all public fields and has no hidden-field claim. Every comparison requires independently selected context; none manufactures an authenticated selection from candidate bytes. Missing entire required component is MissingComponent, never an empty value. Decoder order is caller support (UnsupportedEdition inability), required presence (MissingComponent), available complete-byte allowance (UnavailableBudget), canonical complete framing subject to the read-allowance order of (`rule:identity:component-allowance-order`) (Canonical), closed shape/types/ranges and fixed representation widths in array order (Shape), expected local edition and kind (Edition then Kind: any well-typed unsigned edition or kind other than the expected value has that cause, not Shape; Shape checks its unsigned type/range, not the enumeration), nested grammar and admitted member checks in array order (Field, or Field with the nested component's own Edition or Kind), then collection order and duplicates (Order, Duplicate). Ref text nonemptiness, its 255-UTF-8-byte bound and its label grammar are checked together at Field, at every occurrence; Ref array arity, text types and unsigned edition range are Shape. Fixed representation widths, including the 32-byte run and hash and the 36-byte prevout, remain Shape. History_scope nonemptiness is likewise Field at every occurrence in Context, Scope and PriorRange; its byte allowance is charged at its header under (`rule:identity:component-allowance-order`). Index types and unsigned ranges are checked at Shape; the common decoder assigns no separate index-target Reference cause in these five schemas. Any already admitted nested member retains its own checks at the enclosing Field stage, with a failed member predicate reported as bare Field. For these five schemas the closed set of nested causes is Edition and Kind only: a nested PaperCaseDeclaration, LocalAuthorityDeclaration or AuthorityAccount with its own well-typed but unexpected edition or kind reports Field(Edition) or Field(Kind). Ref, Context, Scope, PriorRange, actor parts, associations and ActId are member grammar, not independently nested component operations. A Field is not wrapped in another Field, and no inability is wrapped. Canonical framing and Shape cover the complete selected value, including inline nested components, and report bare Canonical or Shape; collection Order and Duplicate report bare Order or Duplicate at their enclosing stage. These schemas contain no nested boundary component with its own ordered collections, so they admit no Field(Order), Field(Duplicate), Field(Canonical), Field(Shape) or recursive nested-cause form. The local boundary’s part_index and the index in account_reference wait for the roster stage of (`amend:identity:local-authority-boundary`), after cardinality and part-domain coverage and before any dereference. Order rejects descending adjacent keys; after that check Duplicate rejects equal adjacent keys, so neither condition aliases the other. Compare integer indexes numerically and text keys by their UTF-8 bytes. At each stage complete it before the next; typed construction performs the corresponding size/shape checks but has no supplied-byte Canonical cause. An unchecked decoder result establishes no admission. Contextual comparison follows independent context availability (UnavailableContext), authenticated selection (UnavailableSelection or proven SelectionMismatch), exact context (Context), complete selected descriptor equality (DeclarationContent), then effective scope (Scope: announcement no later than effect, effect no later than this epoch, and no independently recorded end before this epoch). For a declaration, Context compares the run and history_scope within scope; scope timing is checked only at Scope. A changed descriptor, selected edition, run, scope or expectation makes a previous comparison inapplicable, without rewriting any recorded object. Future case proof languages require separate admission and a supported evaluator before activation. No new namespace in the interchange registry, complete-publication framing, digest or signature role is assigned. For a proposed descriptor digest, adjudication on 2026-11-21 stops at the equality-already-given branch of (`alg:identity:adjudication`): complete typed equality and authenticated selection already supply the named decision, under (`crit:identity:benefit`) and (`case:identity:no-identity`). Retake before independently distributed declarations require equality not supplied by that complete comparison. This is the stop record under (`req:identity:stop-record`), not a digest assurance claim. C8 must deliver producer and comparison consumer together; C12, after complete C7P publication binding and crypto evidence, owns enabling. The two C8 commits supply separately selected pure producers and conditional comparison consumers with finite controls, bounded scans of complete supplied layouts and a separately selected current-account query; the real evaluator remains owed. Independent vectors are specified with (`amend:identity:local-authority-boundary`); fixture recomputation admits no implementation.

**Rule (Component read-allowance order)** · `rule:identity:component-allowance-order` This rule applies only to the five component admissions sharing (`amend:identity:paper-case-declaration`). After caller support and required presence, check the known complete input length against the complete-byte allowance; a standalone AuthorityAccount also checks that length against account bytes before canonical framing. During the subsequent bounded framing traversal, check each applicable allowance at the first header that exposes its demand, before allocating, copying or visiting the bounded body or elements. Starting-context bytes bound each history_scope byte-string occurrence in Context, Scope and PriorRange; declarations and transitions bound their respective paper-boundary array counts; parts and associations bound their respective local-boundary array counts; blocks bound the account's block count, and block bytes bound the checked sum of the raw block-byte lengths in that account, excluding CBOR heads. Account bytes bound the complete encoded account subtree, including its heads, at the account's nested position as well as at a standalone entry. Charge an embedded account's span incrementally, reserving a known string body before consuming it and refusing as soon as its encoded extent would exceed the account allowance; do not materialize or re-encode it to find its size. Repeated references do not reset or multiply a per-account sum, and repeated history_scope values each meet the same per-occurrence bound; the complete-byte allowance also bounds their total carriage.

The bounded traversal follows encoded array order. A readable definite header exposes its count or length before its shortest-head and body checks: an exceeded allowance at that header returns UnavailableBudget before those checks, while an unreadable header that supplies no demand returns Canonical. An earlier framing refusal is final; no traversal looks ahead through it for a later budget refusal. If the wire type does not supply the expected count or byte length, no such allowance demand is invented; canonical framing continues within the complete-byte allowance and the closed Shape pass decides the type fault. Schema positions are those of the independently selected operation, not meanings supplied by the candidate. Canonical success is followed by the complete Shape, local Edition/Kind, Field, Order and Duplicate stages already recorded. The reason for early allowance checks is that inability to materialize a value must be decided before spending the resource it bounds; it is not a judgment of that value's later semantic predicates. The same rule and UnavailableBudget outcome apply at nested positions without a new allowance or a nested error wrapper.

Prior-history traversal is not decoding the PriorRange descriptor. Its consumer checks the allowance when it reaches the History stage of (`amend:identity:authority-account`), after that stage's preceding context and binding checks and before obtaining or visiting the independently selected complete earlier history. A known required epoch count is reserved before traversal; otherwise each next epoch is charged before it is visited. The shared account's one required earlier range is charged once for that traversal, not once for each part or association that names the same range. An exhausted traversal returns UnavailableBudget to the caller and establishes no history failure. Arithmetic used only to account for a reader allowance is checked against its remaining allowance without wrapping; inability to represent the demand within the reader's finite capacity is UnavailableBudget, not a new input fault. This does not change the admitted unsigned field ranges, the account's contextual Overflow cause, or any component size formula.

The landed fragment codecs retain the complete-byte and standalone account entry guards and propagate nested allowance exhaustion directly as UnavailableBudget; nested component causes are closed to Edition and Kind. They materialize the complete value before every inner count and byte allowance is checked, with those checks following Shape and the outer Edition and Kind. A PaperCaseDeclaration with kind 2 and a Scope history_scope over its allowance therefore receives Kind; with an invalid Ref namespace instead of that kind defect it receives Field. A LocalAuthorityBoundary with too many parts and a 35-byte prevout in its first part receives Shape; a PaperCaseBoundary with too many active declarations and a non-shortest head at that count or later receives Canonical. The read-allowance rule requires UnavailableBudget for those overlaps instead. The contextual history consumer does not yet consume its declared prior-history allowance. The second C8 commit owes bounded traversal before materialization and prior-history charging at the existing consuming stage under (`rule:identity:component-allowance-order`), with independent overlap controls; the present finite controls do not establish those timing obligations.

The following component CDDL fixes value framing only; its .size controls on Ref text restate the Field bounds of the common discipline, not Shape. Contextual restrictions, grammar, independently supplied finite allowances and first-cause orders remain the five records below, and complete publication CDDL is held for C7P.

```cddl
u64 = uint .le 18446744073709551615
ref = [namespace: tstr .size (1..255), name: tstr .size (1..255), edition: u64]
context = [run: bstr .size 32, history_scope: bstr, epoch: u64]
scope = [run: bstr .size 32, history_scope: bstr, announced_at: u64, effective_at: u64]
prior-range = [history_scope: bstr, first_epoch: 0, exclusive_end: u64]
paper-declaration = [1, ref, 0, scope, ref, ref, ref, ref, [ref, ref]] / [1, ref, 1, scope, ref, ref, ref, ref, [ref, null]]
paper-boundary = [1, context, [* paper-declaration], [* change]]
change = [0, ref, ref] / [1, ref]
local-declaration = [1, ref, scope, [ref, bstr .size 32], ref, ref, ref, bstr]
pin = [height: u64, block_hash: bstr .size 32, buffer: u64]
account = [1, context, pin, [run_configuration: bstr, chain_parameters: bstr], [first_height: u64, blocks: [* bstr]], prior-range]
issuance-locator = [height: u64, transaction_index: u64, input_index: u64, issuance_prevout: bstr .size 36]
actor-part = [1, actor: tstr, issuance-locator, scan_from: u64, scan_through: u64, prior-range]
act-id = ["act", actor: tstr, sequence: u64, family: tstr]
association = [1, context, act_position: u64, act-id, ref, [context, 0], part_index: u64]
local-boundary = ["org.peer-network.test.publication.author-authority", 1, context, local-declaration, account, [* actor-part], [* association]]
```

**Amendment (Paper case boundary admission)** · `amend:identity:paper-case-boundary` Admit PaperCaseBoundary [1,Context,active,changes], with the common framing, limits, decoder order and non-claims of (`amend:identity:paper-case-declaration`). pn-epoch owns and produces the boundary; restoration/replay consumes complete comparison against independently selected declaration history and predecessor. The benefit is explicit completeness and prospective transition evidence. Active is the complete array of PaperCaseDeclaration values, strictly ordered by namespace UTF-8 bytes, name UTF-8 bytes and numeric edition, unique by Ref. Changes is the complete predecessor delta sorted by old Ref: [0,old_ref,new_ref] is a successor and [1,old_ref] a withdrawal. A successor is independently selected, in active, of the same case family and effective at this boundary. Every ceased old declaration has exactly one successor or withdrawal, never both; no change names a non-predecessor or an old binding still active. New adoption is justified by independent declaration history, not an invented old Ref. First publication compares with the selected initial declaration history. Active=[] is an explicit value; an absent member refuses Shape or MissingComponent, not emptiness. Initially active=[] and changes=[]; after withdrawal to emptiness the withdrawals remain. Exact size is H(4)+H(1)+E(Context)+H(len(active))+sum E(declaration)+H(len(changes))+sum E(change). Counts are bounded by the supplied declaration and transition allowances; no unchecked length allocates. After the common decoder and context/selection checks, compare descriptor contents in active order (DeclarationContent), announcement/effect (Scope), full expected active set (CaseSet), predecessor availability (UnavailablePredecessor), then exact successor/withdrawal delta in old-Ref order (Transition). Only then evaluate separately admitted case evidence in active order; missing or demonstrably invalid carried evidence is FailedCase with its predicate, while unavailable rules/evaluator or unevaluable evidence is UnavailableEvaluation, not success or a discrepancy. This declaration envelope does not admit the proof language of a nonempty production case. The deployment currently selects no paper case; its local authority never satisfies one. The admitted transition scope retains its complete in-force case set and successor or withdrawal records without asserting publication binding or an external case’s truth; network-v0.37.4 ``post:epoch:computed-edge-set`` no longer imposes that declaration-set contract. Earlier pins remain unchanged. A changed predecessor, declaration, epoch or selection makes comparison inapplicable. All members are public. For an extra boundary digest, adjudication on 2026-11-21 stops at equality already given: complete typed comparison and the required parent binding supply the decision under (`req:identity:stop-record`); retake (`alg:identity:adjudication`) before independent persistence or authentication loses that complete parent binding. This admits no implemented verifier or publication coordinate. The C8/C12 deadlines and C7P binding hold of (`amend:identity:paper-case-declaration`) apply. Its pure typed producer, fragment codec and conditional comparison consumer now have finite representation and order controls; independently authenticated case selection and nonempty case proof evaluation remain owed. The act-and-epoch document concept (`spec:actepoch:concept`) decides the designed and unbuilt successor for the document subjects stated here; this carrier keeps all its required material and admitted meaning until its owners admit the replacement in the successor document admission cut.

**Amendment (Local authority declaration admission)** · `amend:identity:local-authority-declaration` Admit LocalAuthorityDeclaration [1,Ref,scope,second_party,rules,authorization_convention,projection,run_configuration], with the common framing/decoder of (`amend:identity:paper-case-declaration`). pn-records owns the typed descriptor, independently selected run configuration produces it, and epoch/development-profile comparison consumes it. Ref uses namespace org.peer-network.test.authority, name author-token and edition 1 for this convention; it is not a paper-case constructor. Scope has the common four-member shape. Second_party is [party_ref,genesis_hash], with Ref and exact 32-byte display-order genesis hash; rules, authorization_convention and projection are Ref values. Run_configuration is a byte string of the exact canonical five-member configuration of (`dec:devnet:elements`), checked by its own admitted rules and byte-equal to the independently selected configuration; no run byte changes here. The convention selects PNAT2, 01/81 spends, PNAK1, signing-key-hash/v1, prospective retirement and the six finite run limits; the separate rules edition includes signed-chain validation. These are the existing meanings of (`dec:devnet:author-authority-case`), (`rule:identity:author-chain-view`), (`rule:devnet:author-token`) and (`rule:devnet:author-key-output`). In particular the admission qualification at the chain-view owner remains: the paper's closure verifies recorded signatures only, and local authorization is mandatory for this deployment, not a bound paper case. The benefit is exact selection of the local predicate and its premises without candidate-selected authority. The extension is required local admission material, neither an optional vendor specification in the tagged paper's sense, vendored source, a B3 extension nor an annotation. Exact size is H(8)+H(1)+E(Ref)+E(scope)+E(second_party)+E(rules)+E(authorization_convention)+E(projection)+E(run_configuration); the complete-byte allowance bounds the run configuration, and the starting-context bytes allowance bounds each history_scope; inside an account, the account-byte allowance also bounds the configuration as part of the complete account subtree. After the common decoder, require independent context and authenticated selection, then compare Ref and second-party identity (Declaration), rules (Rules), convention/projection (Convention), complete run configuration (Run), and complete mandatory in-force scope (Scope), in that order. Declaration does not compare the later fields early; those checks together compare the entire descriptor. Unsupported selected rules or convention are UnavailableEvaluation; malformed supported data retain their representation refusal. Changed rules or convention requires a newly selected declaration, not reinterpretation under the same Ref. The explicit deployment transition of (`dec:devnet:elements`) owns local succession; no normal withdrawal removes mandatory local authority, and no predecessor field is inferred from the paper delta. Missing, failing or unevaluable context admits no act. All fields are public. For an extra declaration digest, adjudication on 2026-11-21 stops at equality already given by full descriptor comparison and independent selection under (`req:identity:stop-record`); retake (`alg:identity:adjudication`) before an independently transferred declaration needs equality those operations do not supply. No crypto operation, coordinate or implementation is admitted. C8 produces and compares together; C12 alone selects v3 material for production after C7P and the retained enablement holds. Its pure typed producer, fragment codec and conditional comparison consumer now have finite representation and order controls; authenticated selection and the real signed-chain evaluator remain owed. The lineage concept (`spec:lineage:concept`) decides the designed and unbuilt successor for the token, authority, burn or marker subject stated here; this statement retains its landed or admitted scope until that successor is admitted, and the successor admission cut remains owed by this statement's owner.

**Amendment (Authority account admission)** · `amend:identity:authority-account` Admit AuthorityAccount [1,Context,pin,run_evidence,chain_evidence,prior_record_range]. pn-epoch owns this public evidence carrier, acquisition and epoch assembly produce it, and the development-profile consumer checks it against independent context and prior authenticated records. The benefit is a complete inspectable account shared across acts, not a supplied active-key assertion. Common framing, reader allowances and decoder order are (`amend:identity:paper-case-declaration`). Pin=[h,block_hash,b], with u64 h and b and the exact 32-byte display-order hash. Run_evidence=[run_configuration,chain_parameters], both byte strings retaining exact independently selected public bytes; the first equals (`amend:identity:local-authority-declaration`)'s configuration, the second uses the selected external rules' parameter format. Unsupported format is unavailable evaluation, not guessed normalization. Chain_evidence=[s,blocks] holds complete raw blocks in consecutive height order from s through t=h+b inclusive. For a nonempty covered-Actor domain s equals the minimum qualified issuance height of the local boundary's parts; each issuance is at or before h. For no new acts, s=h and blocks contains the pin and its b successors, while parts and associations are explicitly empty. Genesis remains a selected identity and parameter, not a demand to carry every block since genesis. Prior_record_range=[history_scope,0,epoch] refers to the complete supplied authenticated earlier epoch record; no filtered retirement list or external URL replaces it. Earlier retirements use their original epoch account and pin. C7P owns binding to those records and the complete publication; a fixture premise does not supply it. Its pure typed producer, fragment codec and comparison under explicit synthetic evaluator premises now have finite representation and order controls; complete account acquisition, chain validation and authenticated selection remain owed. The lineage concept (`spec:lineage:concept`) decides the designed and unbuilt successor for the token, authority, burn or marker subject stated here; this statement retains its landed or admitted scope until that successor is admitted, and the successor admission cut remains owed by this statement's owner.

The account follows (`rule:devnet:author-public-evidence`)'s complete issuance-to-prefix blocks and (`rule:identity:author-key-history`)'s consensus-validity premise. Check header linkage, transaction commitments and declared signed-chain evidence under the selected rules; do not add full consensus replay from genesis. Each part scans all transactions from its own qualified issuance through h using checked offsets height−s; unrelated transactions inside that span count, earlier shared blocks and buffer do not enter its authority set. A retirement boundary narrows contributing declarations, not the required token-path scan. Shared storage does not discount per-Actor history or complete-scan limits. No supplied path list, key list, current tip or UTXO assertion replaces that scan. The run's declaration_outputs, history_bytes, history_transactions, active_entries, scan_blocks and scan_bytes retain their exact meanings and selected values. Complete account bytes and buffer are additionally charged against the reader's finite allowances. Compute t=h+b, N=t−s+1 with checked u64 arithmetic after s≤h. E(chain_evidence)=H(2)+H(s)+H(N)+sum from j=s through t of [H(len(block_j))+len(block_j)]. E(pin)=H(3)+H(h)+H(32)+32+H(b). E(account)=H(6)+H(1)+E(Context)+E(pin)+E(run_evidence)+E(chain_evidence)+E(prior_record_range). No omitted framing or hidden fixed ceiling exists. Unrepresentable scalar/length is Shape; contextual h+b or interval overflow is Overflow; insufficient reader allowance is UnavailableBudget, never a false membership result. After the common decoder and independent context, check selected prefix equality (Prefix) then checked interval arithmetic (Overflow), required carried members/count completeness (MissingEvidence under the version-three amendment of (`rule:devnet:author-public-evidence`)), supported evaluator (UnavailableRules), independent public-data/prior-record binding (Binding if disproved, UnavailableBinding if not established), consecutive header/transaction/signed-chain evidence (Chain on demonstrated failure, UnavailableEvaluation otherwise), prefix/stability (Prefix), present prior epochs/contexts (History for a present but mismatched epoch or context, never an omission), each part's issuance and asset in Actor order (Qualification/Actor), and derived s/scan bounds (Coverage for a wrong claimed minimum or scan bound that omits no required evidence), then each complete token scan and original-epoch retirement evaluation. At whichever stage first demonstrates an omission of a required block, prior epoch, act evidence or retirement placement, return MissingEvidence and do not continue to a later cause; discovering the omission later does not rename it History or Coverage. In particular a first height above a required qualified issuance omits required blocks and is MissingEvidence even when its own carried interval count is consistent; a first height below the minimum with every required block present is Coverage. The earlier ordered checks still precede any omission not yet demonstrated. Existing unsupported-path and run-ceiling outcomes stay unavailable; no inability becomes ActorBinding. MissingEvidence refuses the supplied record's completeness while authority remains unavailable, not unauthorized. Malformed issuance/path and proven membership failures retain their distinct causes. Candidate missing evidence is never silently replaced by a fetched current chain. Changed pin, scope, part domain, rules, account or prior history makes comparison inapplicable; later chain state never rewrites this pin, and replacement of a settled prefix retains the run halt. All fields are public and no external authority/finality/consensus fact follows from equality. For an extra account digest, adjudication on 2026-11-21 stops at equality already given by complete value and parent comparison under (`req:identity:stop-record`); retake (`alg:identity:adjudication`) before a standalone transferable account certificate or cross-boundary reference needs equality beyond complete authenticated records and scoped indexes. This design implements neither chain acquisition nor a verifier; C8 owns producer/comparison together and C7P/C12 retain binding and enablement.

**Amendment (Local authority boundary admission)** · `amend:identity:local-authority-boundary` Admit LocalAuthorityBoundary [discriminator,1,Context,declaration,account,parts,associations], with discriminator org.peer-network.test.publication.author-authority and semantic required member name local_author_authority. pn-epoch owns and assembles it; fresh admission and restoration consume the same independently selected required_boundary_context under (`amend:design:epoch-authentication-restoration`): publication operation, run/history/epoch, expected host, marker prefix, authenticated declarations, prior verified record and supported evaluators, with no default. The benefit is exact association of each first-accepted act with its local authority evidence, distinct from closure signature verification and from paper-case evaluation. It is required local material as defined by (`dec:devnet:author-authority-case`), not optional annotations, optional vendor specifications, vendored source or verification_cases. The discriminator identifies this component schema, not a complete interchange namespace or coordinate; a value other than the exact stated text refuses Shape at that first array member, before Edition. A closure-only reader may leave the local predicate unevaluated while reporting its separate closure result; it cannot call that result deployment admission, discard required bytes while claiming equality, or guess an unknown publication theory. MissingComponent, malformed representation, candidate Edition mismatch and unsupported selected edition inability retain the common order of (`amend:identity:paper-case-declaration`). The paper case set never supplies this component, even when empty. All fields are public; no D, removal fact or courtesy result is read. Its pure typed producer, fragment codec and conditional comparison consumer have finite representation and order controls; conditional scans over complete supplied account layouts and original-epoch retirement evidence are implemented, while authenticated binding and the real evaluator remain owed. The lineage concept (`spec:lineage:concept`) decides the designed and unbuilt successor for the token, authority, burn or marker subject stated here; this statement retains its landed or admitted scope until that successor is admitted, and the successor admission cut remains owed by this statement's owner. The payload family (`conv:devnet:wire-boundary`) designs an unbuilt successor that retains independently selected authority context and holds the successor binding of that context in signed acts and epoch documents; this statement retains its landed or admitted meaning until replacement, and the identity and development-profile owners owe the coordinated payload and authority admission cut.

Declaration and account are exactly (`amend:identity:local-authority-declaration`) and (`amend:identity:authority-account`). Parts are unique and sorted by existing Actor order and cover precisely Actors of first-accepted acts at this boundary, not every possible Actor. Each member is [1,Actor,issuance_locator,scan_from,scan_through,prior_record_range], with unchanged canonical author text, issuance_locator=[height,transaction_index,input_index,issuance_prevout], u64 indexes and exact 36-byte consensus-order prevout; qualification derives the asset and compares Actor before authority. Scan_from equals that Actor's qualified issuance height, scan_through=h, and prior_record_range equals the account's full range. The initial 1 in these member shapes is a fixed layout marker governed by the enclosing edition, not an independent lifecycle. Associations are [1,Context,act_position,ActId,local_declaration_ref,account_reference,part_index], exactly one per first-accepted act in authoritative position order, with no new association for a historical act. ActId is the exact third-formation value; account_reference=[Context,0] names the sole shared account in this boundary; part_index is checked before access. Context and ActId together select the complete retained ClosureAct, including exact V, material, signatures and placement, not an unchecked identifier lookup. Its part must name that Actor and the local declaration, account and context must match. V fixes P and proposal signature, material fixes the one Actor key and run, and the carrier retains seal and approval; no redundant copies or new message role are introduced. A shared signer key never determines Actor identity. A lookup outside the covered domain is NotCovered inability, not unauthorized. Empty parts/associations for no new acts assert no global absence of keys. Part and association are admitted under this object by (`red:identity:fields-to-object`), with no separate class, digest or stop.

Exact boundary size is H(7)+E(discriminator)+H(1)+E(Context)+E(declaration)+E(account)+H(len(parts))+sum E(part)+H(len(associations))+sum E(association). E(part)=H(6)+H(1)+E(Actor)+E(issuance_locator)+H(scan_from)+H(scan_through)+E(prior_record_range), E(issuance_locator)=H(4)+H(height)+H(transaction_index)+H(input_index)+H(36)+36. E(association)=H(7)+H(1)+E(Context)+H(act_position)+E(ActId)+E(local_declaration_ref)+E(account_reference)+H(part_index). Ref and Actor bounds and existing nested identity limits apply exactly; part/association counts and total bytes are checked against finite reader allowances and the independently supplied complete first-acceptance roster. After common decoding, check available independently selected context, authenticated selection, Context, then Declaration, Rules, Convention, Run and Scope; compare settled prefix/buffer and checked arithmetic (Prefix/Overflow or UnavailableLinkage); compare roster cardinality and part-domain coverage, then indexes (Coverage/Reference, not yet derived issuance or per-act association equality); finish each retained act's admitted header/material comparisons; validate the account in its full stated order; then per association compare act position/ActId (Act), Actor (Actor), account (Account), part (Part), existing signature roles under the independently selected expected host, and finally signing-key-hash/v1 membership in H(a,k) (ActorBinding only on proven nonmembership). H uses valid path declarations from the effective retirement transaction inclusive, or issuance if none, through h. All acts of k use the set at k's start; retirement acts of k affect k+1 only under (`rule:identity:retirement-order`). Context mismatch makes the comparison inapplicable; an invalid signature and an unauthorized key remain distinct. The full consumer returns separate closure, local-authority and paper-case results; unavailable is neither confirmed nor failed membership. A successful local comparison does not authenticate finalized inclusion: (`open:design:dependency-opening-recipe`) retains that hold and no association manufactures an anchor. For an extra boundary digest, adjudication on 2026-11-21 stops at equality already given by complete value, scoped references and parent binding under (`req:identity:stop-record`); retake (`alg:identity:adjudication`) before independent persistence/authentication loses that binding or a field acquires an independent lifecycle. C8 must supply producers and comparison consumers together; C7P assigns complete publication framing/binding and C12 enables and switches material selection. No implementation, signature operation or publication coordinate is assigned here.

The five component families have independent public inputs, canonical bytes, exact size checks, substitutions and expected future outcomes in packages/act-authentication/tests/boundary-golden.json, reproduced by packages/act-authentication/tests/boundary_vectors.py using only the Python standard library, never package encoders or validators. Write with python3 -B packages/act-authentication/tests/boundary_vectors.py --write packages/act-authentication/tests/boundary-golden.json and compare with python3 -B packages/act-authentication/tests/boundary_vectors.py < packages/act-authentication/tests/boundary-golden.json. The named synthetic chain/rules and binding contexts are explicit fixture premises, not Elements validation, consensus, authenticity or finalized order; C8 supplies conditional component producers and consumers, shared issuance-span scans and registered controls under explicit evaluator, consensus and binding premises; C8E in (`preview:design:authentication-cuts`) owes the real selected evaluator, signed-chain evidence checks and registered integration controls. This split lets the later binding and switch work consume the component surface and bounded scans before cryptographic integration is admitted; keeping the evaluator inside C8 would instead delay that whole surface until both operation admissions close. C8’s assessment results, not the admitted component values, retain their evaluator selection and premises, and production authority evaluation remains unavailable until C8E and the required binding are available. C12 still waits for the real evaluator: neither this staging nor a fixture weakens (`dec:devnet:author-authority-case`) or (`rule:identity:author-chain-view`). Whichever of C8E and C10 verifies first owns the fixed-message operation admission under (`open:crypto:dependency-admission`), with independent cryptographic integration review; C8E owns the signed-chain operation admission. This preserves the two cuts’ independent sequencing because courtesy role verification does not need the signed-chain evaluator; making C10 wait for C8E would add that unrelated delay without strengthening either admission. In both cuts, recorded-role verification consumes the fixed-message candidate of (`amend:crypto:public-verification-admission`) only after its review and import, while the signed-chain challenge evaluator needs its own new operation admission and independent vectors. Neither route enables a verifier or signer before its admission. Exact byte lengths are independently counted by formulas and encoding; no checksum or component digest is assigned. Nonzero start heights, different issuances, full per-Actor scans, buffer exclusion, empty-domain support, retirement history, first-cause overlap and inability are retained for future consumers. Named overlaps retain budget before canonical framing for all five families, Edition before Kind, Order before Duplicate, Duplicate before conflicting Transition, Declaration before Rules, Prefix before Overflow, and roster Coverage before Reference. Other combined faults, including selected-evaluator and late-scan combinations requiring execution of external rules, remain C8 registered controls under explicit evaluator premises; their execution under the real selected rules belongs to C8E’s integration controls; these representation fixtures do not claim that every semantic combination is executable here. No fixture recomputation admits an implementation, advances a claim or removes an enablement hold. A changed component representation requires a fresh component edition, never reinterpretation of historical carriers.
